									<p>My husband and I are doing all we can to increase our retirement portfolio. There’s no better return on your money like Real Estate. Our goal is to have an ADU on every one of our rental properties. After building our first two ADU’s, the traditional way, we found <span class="alt">Samara</span>. I can successfully make the statement that going with <span class="alt">Samara</span> was, Hand’s Down, the best decision we’ve gone with this far! My husband and I have a mortgage company and we knew we wanted to finance our next ADU. The finance option that was quoted to us was so low we couldn’t even touch the interest rate.
